[basemainpkd.py] class PvtV2B4_FPN_PKD_SKD(PvtV2B4_FPN_Baseline):
    """Plain FPN baseline with prompt KD and scale KD training losses.

    The encoder, FPN, prediction head, parameter groups, and inference path
    are exactly those of :class:`PvtV2B4_FPN_Baseline`.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        pkd_weight=0.5,
        skd_weight=1.0,
        pkd_erode_kernel=5,
        pkd_min_valid_pixels=16,
        skd_view="image_s",
        skd_ramp_start=0.0,
        skd_ramp_end=0.0,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        super().__init__(**kwargs)

        self.pkd_weight = float(pkd_weight)
        self.skd_weight = float(skd_weight)
        self.pkd_erode_kernel = int(pkd_erode_kernel)
        self.pkd_min_valid_pixels = int(pkd_min_valid_pixels)
        self.skd_view = str(skd_view)
        self.skd_ramp_start = float(skd_ramp_start)
        self.skd_ramp_end = float(skd_ramp_end)

        if self.pkd_weight < 0 or self.skd_weight < 0:
            raise ValueError("pkd_weight and skd_weight must be non-negative.")
        if self.pkd_erode_kernel < 1 or self.pkd_erode_kernel % 2 == 0:
            raise ValueError("pkd_erode_kernel must be a positive odd integer.")
        if self.pkd_min_valid_pixels < 1:
            raise ValueError("pkd_min_valid_pixels must be positive.")
        if self.skd_view not in {"image_s", "image_l"}:
            raise ValueError("skd_view must be either 'image_s' or 'image_l'.")

    @staticmethod
    def _resize_nearest(tensor, size):
        if tensor.shape[-2:] == size:
            return tensor
        return F.interpolate(tensor.float(), size=size, mode="nearest")

    def _build_online_pkd_target(self, data, mask, output_size):
        """Build reliable PKD labels from the pseudo-mask and box prompt."""
        external_target = data.get("pkd_target")
        external_valid = data.get("pkd_valid")
        if external_target is not None or external_valid is not None:
            if external_target is None or external_valid is None:
                raise KeyError(
                    "External PKD supervision requires both 'pkd_target' "
                    "and 'pkd_valid'."
                )
            target = self._resize_nearest(external_target, output_size).clamp(0, 1)
            valid = self._resize_nearest(external_valid, output_size) > 0.5
            return target, valid

        if "box_mask" not in data:
            raise KeyError(
                "PKD is enabled but data['box_mask'] is missing. Set "
                "train.data.use_box=True and configure box_json in dataset.yaml."
            )

        box = self._resize_nearest(data["box_mask"], output_size) > 0.5
        binary_mask = self._resize_nearest(mask, output_size) > 0.5

        # Binary erosion implemented as max-pooling the inverted mask.  The
        # core avoids uncertain pseudo-label boundaries and is clipped by the
        # weak box prompt.  Pixels outside all boxes are certain background.
        inverted = (~binary_mask).float()
        eroded_foreground = 1.0 - F.max_pool2d(
            inverted,
            kernel_size=self.pkd_erode_kernel,
            stride=1,
            padding=self.pkd_erode_kernel // 2,
        )
        foreground_core = (eroded_foreground > 0.5) & box
        outside_box = ~box

        target = foreground_core.float()
        valid = foreground_core | outside_box
        return target, valid

    def _prompt_kd_loss(self, logits, data, mask):
        target, valid = self._build_online_pkd_target(
            data=data,
            mask=mask,
            output_size=logits.shape[-2:],
        )
        valid_float = valid.float()
        valid_per_sample = valid_float.flatten(1).sum(dim=1)
        usable = valid_per_sample >= self.pkd_min_valid_pixels

        if not bool(usable.any()):
            zero = logits.sum() * 0.0
            return zero, zero.detach()

        pixel_loss = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits,
            target,
            reduction="none",
        )
        selected = usable[:, None, None, None].float()
        weights = valid_float * selected
        loss = (pixel_loss * weights).sum() / weights.sum().clamp_min(1.0)
        valid_ratio = weights.mean()
        return loss, valid_ratio.detach()

    def _skd_coefficient(self, iter_percentage):
        if self.skd_ramp_end <= self.skd_ramp_start:
            return 1.0
        progress = float(iter_percentage)
        progress = min(max(progress, 0.0), 1.0)
        return min(
            max(
                (progress - self.skd_ramp_start)
                / (self.skd_ramp_end - self.skd_ramp_start),
                0.0,
            ),
            1.0,
        )

    def forward(self, data, iter_percentage=1, **kwargs):
        del kwargs
        logits = self.body(data=data)

        # Evaluation stays identical to the baseline: no box and no second
        # scale are required at inference time.
        if not self.training:
            return logits

        mask = self._resize_nearest(data["mask"].float(), logits.shape[-2:])
        bce_loss = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(logits, mask, reduction="mean")

        if self.pkd_weight > 0:
            pkd_loss, pkd_valid_ratio = self._prompt_kd_loss(
                logits=logits,
                data=data,
                mask=mask,
            )
        else:
            pkd_loss = logits.sum() * 0.0
            pkd_valid_ratio = pkd_loss.detach()

        skd_coef = self._skd_coefficient(iter_percentage)
        if self.skd_weight > 0 and skd_coef > 0:
            if self.skd_view not in data:
                raise KeyError(
                    f"SKD is enabled but data['{self.skd_view}'] is missing."
                )
            scale_logits = self.body(data={"image_m": data[self.skd_view]})
            scale_probability = F.interpolate(
                scale_logits.sigmoid(),
                size=logits.shape[-2:],
                mode="bilinear",
                align_corners=False,
            )
            teacher_probability = logits.sigmoid().detach()
            skd_loss = F.l1_loss(
                scale_probability,
                teacher_probability,
                reduction="mean",
            )
        else:
            skd_loss = logits.sum() * 0.0

        weighted_pkd = self.pkd_weight * pkd_loss
        weighted_skd = self.skd_weight * skd_coef * skd_loss
        total_loss = bce_loss + weighted_pkd + weighted_skd

        detached = {
            "bce": bce_loss.detach(),
            "pkd": pkd_loss.detach(),
            "skd": skd_loss.detach(),
            "pkd_weighted": weighted_pkd.detach(),
            "skd_weighted": weighted_skd.detach(),
            "pkd_valid_ratio": pkd_valid_ratio,
            "skd_coef": logits.new_tensor(skd_coef),
            "total": total_loss.detach(),
        }
        return {
            "logits": logits,
            "vis": {"sal": logits.sigmoid()},
            "loss": total_loss,
            "loss_items": detached,
            "loss_str": (
                f"L:{detached['total'].item():.4f} "
                f"BCE:{detached['bce'].item():.4f} "
                f"PKD:{detached['pkd'].item():.4f} "
                f"SKD:{detached['skd'].item():.4f}"
            ),
        }
